D26 removes Production account-table reads from the Replay chart endpoint. D27 classifies an open position at `executionEnd` as a censored lifecycle, never a successful exit match. The fixed-notional boundary remains binding: Replay must not use `wallet_settings`, cash, daily budget, position-cap, or wallet-sizing policy to decide its output; those current reads remain D13. All three are implementation blockers. No application code was changed.

514:    private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
515:                                      int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
516:        int resolved = 0;
517:        int size = candles.size();
518:        for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
519:            checkStopRequested(runId);
520:            Candle candle = candles.get(i);
521:            List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
522:                    symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
523:            if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
524:                resolved++;
525:            }
526:            if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
527:                int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
528:                updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
529:                        "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
530:            }
531:        }
532:        return resolved;
533:    }

118:    public List<PriceEvent> find(String rawSymbol, Instant startInclusive, Instant endInclusive) {
119:        String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
120:        return jdbcTemplate.query("""
121:                SELECT observed_at, price, source
122:                FROM market_price_event
123:                WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at >= ? AND observed_at <= ?
124:                ORDER BY observed_at ASC, id ASC
125:                """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PriceEvent(
126:                        rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
127:                        rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),
128:                symbol, Timestamp.from(startInclusive), Timestamp.from(endInclusive));
129:    }

133:    public java.util.Optional<PriceEvent> findLatestAtOrBefore(String rawSymbol, Instant reference) {
134:        String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
135:        List<PriceEvent> rows = jdbcTemplate.query("""
136:                SELECT observed_at, price, source
137:                FROM market_price_event
138:                WHERE delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED') AND symbol = ? AND observed_at <= ?
139:                ORDER BY observed_at DESC, id DESC
140:                LIMIT 1

142:                        rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
143:                        rs.getBigDecimal("price"), rs.getString("source")),

148:    /** FIX-132: known skipped/unconfirmed collector delivery must not silently
149:     * become legacy candle-price fallback. Historical pre-collector windows retain
150:     * their established fallback. This reads immutable evidence, never live claims. */
151:    public void assertReplayEvidence(String symbol,Instant from,Instant to) {
152:        Long pending=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status='PENDING'",
153:            Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
154:        if(pending!=null && pending>0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 Replay contains unconfirmed Production price outcomes; reconcile before claiming parity");
155:        // FIX-153 OFF Replay never queries collector-era activation tables.
156:        if("OFF".equals(mode))return;
157:        Long cutovers=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM shared_market_cutover_history WHERE symbol=? AND cutover_at<=?",Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(to));
158:        if(cutovers!=null && cutovers>0) {
159:            Long applied=jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM market_price_event WHERE symbol=? AND observed_at BETWEEN ? AND ? AND delivery_status IN ('LEGACY','APPLIED')",
160:                Long.class,symbol,Timestamp.from(from),Timestamp.from(to));
161:            if(applied==null || applied==0)throw new IllegalStateException("FIX-132 no applied price evidence in collector-era Replay window; signal-price fallback is not Production reproduction");
162:        }
163:    }

32:
33:    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
34:        return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
35:    }
36:
37:    public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
38:                      ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
39:        if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
40:        List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
41:                .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
42:                .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
43:                .toList();
44:        state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
45:        return new Scope();
46:    }
47:
48:    /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
49:    public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
50:        if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
51:        State s=required();
52:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
53:        if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
54:            s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
55:    }
56:    public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
57:        StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
58:        return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
59:    }

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** the draft is internally contradictory about Production’s signal-price exit order. Section 3.3 says `TP → stop loss → rebase floor → profit lock → SELL → near-TP → add`; current Production is `position management → TP → profit-lock trigger → stop loss → rebase hard floor → SELL (suppressed if lock active) → add`. Near-TP is not invoked on this signal path. D16 correctly states most of the actual order, so the draft contradicts itself on a critical-safety fixture.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** Appendix B’s D14 rationale is false when Dynamic Profit Lock is disabled. Production returns inactive before calling `ProfitLockPolicy`; Replay calls the policy with the disabled flag and preserves separate rebase behavior. The required fixture set must include toggle-off and rebase-state cases; “same arguments” cannot support equivalence.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** current Replay has a reachable Binance HTTP dependency through near-TP minimum-execution evaluation. This violates the draft’s stated no-HTTP boundary, but D11 treats it only as a quantity difference and the registry does not explicitly retire/replace it.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** the proposed boundary forbids `wallet_settings`, yet the shared `TradeExecutionValidationService` unconditionally reads it for BUY transition and execution-profile decisions. The draft simultaneously says Replay keeps using that shared service and that no Production class changes. A Replay-local replacement may be possible, but the exact routing and affected methods are not registered, so the current contract is not implementable as written.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** seeded lifecycle comparison is underspecified. The proposed entry fixture covers an initial entry, but Production progressive adds change average entry, stop loss, and take profit. The contract does not define whether adds/partial harvests are seeded, replayed, excluded, or which signal stream drives lifecycle decisions. That permits signal differences to contaminate a supposedly separate lifecycle metric.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** the outcome model is unresolved. `ANALYSIS_PARITY` expressly makes no lifecycle claim, while Sections 7.1–7.2 require lifecycle acceptance thresholds and critical-safety gates. The specification needs a distinct lifecycle-verification result/certificate status, or a clear rule that lifecycle results are non-label certificate fields.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** the boundary includes `RegressionTestService` and its controllers, but D26 only removes the `/runs/{id}/all-trades-chart` account read. The “proven trades” list and detail paths call `TradeInvestmentHistory.enrichProven`, which can query `wallet_trade`. Either those endpoints must be explicitly outside the Regression Replay boundary or be covered by the dependency/removal registry.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Current-code defect supporting redesign:** the current pass condition treats a run as price-parity-capable if *any* accepted price event exists in the window. It does not prove coverage for each entry, add, protection, or final-tail span.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Current-code defect supporting redesign:** `MarketPriceEventService.find` orders by `(observed_at, id)` but projects neither ID nor delivery status into `PriceEvent`; equal-time ordering and evidence status therefore cannot be audited downstream.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** this file is named RPR-0.9.1 but identifies itself as RPR-0.9.4; the canonical `agent-docs/FINAL_CHANGE_SPECIFICATION.md` remains RPR-0.4. The current reviewed draft hash is `4EE4676C…02996A3`, but no canonical, frozen identical revision/source/configuration manifest exists.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — Freeze blocker:** E01–E10 are required before approval, but no attached artifacts with those stated deliverable identifiers were found. In particular, no read-only coverage, configuration-history, dependency, or entry-fixture evidence is available for review.

## OBJECTIONS

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Do not freeze tests from the incorrect Section 3.3 order; doing so could certify wrong stop-loss/profit-lock precedence.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The specification must select one explicit solution for shared-service account reads: Replay-local method mirror, an approved shared-code change, or non-comparability. It cannot leave all three implications active.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The lifecycle denominator can be inflated by excluding difficult cases unless the fixture lifecycle—including adds and partial exits—is defined before measurement.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — correction to a peer claim:** `/runs/{id}/fix127` reading `signal_processing_work` is not itself a D26 account-table violation; the draft expressly permits that table as stored oracle evidence. The proven-trade `wallet_trade` path is the confirmed broader-boundary omission.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No database, Production runtime, credentials, logs, or external websites were accessed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Price-span coverage, historical configuration availability, fixture completeness, and the achievable comparable-case denominator remain unknown until E01–E10 are produced through bounded read-only evidence collection.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The intended treatment of Production adds, partial harvests, and currently open seeded positions is not defined.

## FINDINGS FOR JEAN

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** This is not freeze-ready. Correct the wrong signal-path order; explicitly resolve HTTP and `wallet_settings` dependencies; define seeded-lifecycle mechanics and lifecycle certification; widen or delimit the Replay API boundary; then produce the required E01–E10 evidence.

VERDICT: REJECTED
